# Sampling profile diff

Collected 1,114 → 1,433 samples (+319 samples, +28.6%).

| Category         | Change | Delta |             % |       Samples |
| ---------------- | -----: | ----: | ------------: | ------------: |
| Ours             | +25.5% |  +258 | 90.9% → 88.7% | 1,013 → 1,271 |
| Standard library | +60.4% |   +61 |  9.1% → 11.3% |     101 → 162 |

Call stacks missing their outermost callers account for 28.7% → 33.1% of the samples collected.

`…` stands for omitted frames. `truncated` stands for missing outermost callers.
